00034: def smooth_path(points, subdivisions=5):
00035:     result = []
00036:     for i in range(len(points)-1):
00037:         p0 = points[max(0,i-1)]
00038:         p1 = points[i]
00039:         p2 = points[i+1]
00040:         p3 = points[min(len(points)-1,i+2)]
00041:         for j in range(subdivisions):
00042:             t = j/subdivisions
00043:             q = tuple(.5*((2*p1[k])+(-p0[k]+p2[k])*t+
00044:                 (2*p0[k]-5*p1[k]+4*p2[k]-p3[k])*t*t+
00045:                 (-p0[k]+3*p1[k]-3*p2[k]+p3[k])*t*t*t)
00046:                 for k in range(3))
00047:             if not result or sum((q[k]-result[-1][k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-12:
00048:                 result.append(q)
00049:     result.append(points[-1])
00050:     return result
00051: 
00052: def tube(points, radius, color):
00053:     return sweep_tube(points=smooth_path(points), radius=radius, color=color)
